function updateAuthUI() { const loginBtn = $("googleLoginBtn"); const avatar = $("userAvatar"); if (currentUser) { if (loginBtn) { loginBtn.textContent = "Account"; loginBtn.onclick = () => openProfile(); } if (avatar) { const name = currentUser.displayName || currentUser.email || "U"; avatar.textContent = name.charAt(0).toUpperCase(); avatar.title = name; avatar.style.cursor = "pointer"; avatar.onclick = () => openProfile(); } } else { if (loginBtn) { loginBtn.textContent = "Sign in with Google"; loginBtn.onclick = signInWithGoogle; } if (avatar) { avatar.textContent = "U"; avatar.title = "Sign in"; } } } async function signInWithGoogle() { try { const provider = new firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider(); provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: "select_account" }); await auth.signInWithPopup(provider); } catch (error) { console.error("Google login error:", error); showToast(error.message || "Login failed.", "error"); } } async function signOutUser() { try { await auth.signOut(); showToast("Signed out successfully.", "success"); } catch (error) { console.error("Sign out error:", error); showToast("Unable to sign out.", "error"); } } async function ensureUserProfile(user) { if (!user) return null; const ref = db.collection("users").doc(user.uid); const snap = await ref.get(); const baseProfile = { uid: user.uid, email: user.email || "", displayName: user.displayName || "MemeCoin User", photoURL: user.photoURL || "", updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }; if (!snap.exists) { await ref.set({ ...baseProfile, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), walletBalance: 0, coinsOwned: 0, listingsCount: 0, verified: false, banned: false, role: "user" }); } else { await ref.set(baseProfile, {merge: true}); } const updated = await ref.get(); return updated.data() || baseProfile; } async function loadUserProfile() { if (!currentUser) return null; try { currentProfile = await ensureUserProfile(currentUser); state.walletBalance = Number( currentProfile.walletBalance || 0 ); updateWalletUI(); updateStats(); return currentProfile; } catch (error) { console.error("User profile error:", error); showToast("Unable to load your profile.", "error"); return null; } } function updateWalletUI() { const wallet = $("walletBalance"); if (!wallet) return; wallet.textContent = money(state.walletBalance); } function updateStats() { const coins = $("statCoins"); const listings = $("statListings"); const owned = $("statOwned"); if (coins) { coins.textContent = assetsCache.length.toLocaleString(); } if (listings) { listings.textContent = marketplaceCache.length.toLocaleString(); } if (owned) { owned.textContent = ownedCoinsCache.length.toLocaleString(); } } function setAdminVisibility() { isAdmin = isCurrentAdmin(); document.querySelectorAll(".admin-only").forEach(element => { element.style.display = isAdmin ? "" : "none"; }); document.querySelectorAll("[data-admin-only]").forEach(element => { element.hidden = !isAdmin; }); const adminPage = $("admin"); if (adminPage && !isAdmin) { adminPage.classList.remove("active"); } } async function handleUserStatus() { if (!currentUser) return true; if (!currentProfile) { await loadUserProfile(); } if (currentProfile && currentProfile.banned === true) { showToast("Your account has been restricted.", "error"); await signOutUser(); return false; } return true; } function formatDate(value) { if (!value) return "—"; try { if (value.toDate) { return value.toDate().toLocaleString(); } return new Date(value).toLocaleString(); } catch { return "—"; } } function escapeHTML(value) { return safeText(value) .replace(/&/g, "&") .replace(//g, ">") .replace(/"/g, """) .replace(/'/g, "'"); } function tierClass(tier) { return "tier-" + safeText(tier || "New") .toLowerCase() .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-"); } function tierLabel(tier) { if (!tier) return "New / Unreviewed"; if (tier === "New") return "New / Unreviewed"; return tier; } function getTierRange(tier) { const ranges = { New: [1, 5], Bronze: [5, 25], Silver: [25, 100], Gold: [100, 500], Luxury: [500, 5000] }; return ranges[tier] || ranges.New; } function validTierPrice(tier, price) { const range = getTierRange(tier); const amount = Number(price); return amount >= range[0] && amount <= range[1]; } function generateCoinId() { const chars = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789"; let result = "MC-"; for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { result += chars.charAt( Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length) ); } return result; } function generateListingId() { return "LIST-" + Date.now() + "-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7); } function generateTransactionId() { return "TX-" + Date.now() + "-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8); } function calculateBuyerFee(price) { return Number(price || 0) * PLATFORM_FEE; } function calculateBuyerTotal(price) { const amount = Number(price || 0); return amount + calculateBuyerFee(amount); } function normalizeAsset(data, id) { return { id: id || data.id || "", coinID: data.coinID || id || generateCoinId(), name: data.name || data.coinName || "Unnamed MemeCoin", symbol: data.symbol || "MEME", description: data.description || "", image: data.image || data.imageUrl || "", primaryPrice: Number(data.primaryPrice || data.price || 0), tier: data.tier || "New", supply: Number(data.supply || 0), createdBy: data.createdBy || ADMIN_UID, status: data.status || "active", createdAt: data.createdAt || null }; } function normalizeListing(data, id) { const price = Number(data.price || data.listingPrice || 0); return { id: id || data.id || "", assetId: data.assetId || data.coinID || "", coinID: data.coinID || data.assetId || "", sellerId: data.sellerId || data.ownerId || "", sellerName: data.sellerName || "Seller", price: price, tier: data.tier || "New", status: data.status || "active", createdAt: data.createdAt || null }; } function renderEmpty(container, message) { if (!container) return; container.innerHTML = '
' + escapeHTML(message) + "
"; } function closeAllModals() { document.querySelectorAll(".modal").forEach(modal => { modal.classList.remove("active"); }); } document.addEventListener("keydown", event => { if (event.key === "Escape") { closeAllModals(); } }); document.addEventListener("click", event => { if (event.target.classList.contains("modal")) { event.target.classList.remove("active"); } }); function openProfile() { if (!currentUser) { signInWithGoogle(); return; } renderProfile(); openModal("profileModal"); } function renderProfile() { const container = $("profileContent"); if (!container) return; const profile = currentProfile || {}; const name = profile.displayName || currentUser.displayName || "MemeCoin User"; const email = profile.email || currentUser.email || ""; container.innerHTML = `
${escapeHTML( name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() )}

${escapeHTML(name)}

${escapeHTML(email)}

${ownedCoinsCache.length}Owned
${marketplaceCache.filter( item => item.sellerId === uid() ).length}Listings
${money( state.walletBalance )}Wallet
`; } async function loadAssets() { try { const snapshot = await db.collection("assets") .where("status", "==", "active") .get(); assetsCache = []; snapshot.forEach(doc => { assetsCache.push(normalizeAsset(doc.data(), doc.id)); }); assetsCache.sort((a, b) => { const aTime = a.createdAt?.seconds || 0; const bTime = b.createdAt?.seconds || 0; return bTime - aTime; }); renderFeaturedCoins(); renderMarketplace(); updateAdminCounts(); updateStats(); } catch (error) { console.error("Load assets error:", error); showToast("Unable to load MemeCoins.", "error"); } } async function loadMarketplace() { try { const snapshot = await db.collection("marketplace") .where("status", "==", "active") .get(); marketplaceCache = []; snapshot.forEach(doc => { marketplaceCache.push( normalizeListing(doc.data(), doc.id) ); }); marketplaceCache.sort((a, b) => { const aTime = a.createdAt?.seconds || 0; const bTime = b.createdAt?.seconds || 0; return bTime - aTime; }); renderMarketplace(); renderMyListings(); updateAdminCounts(); updateStats(); } catch (error) { console.error("Load marketplace error:", error); showToast("Unable to load marketplace.", "error"); } } async function loadOwnedCoins() { if (!currentUser) { ownedCoinsCache = []; renderMyCoins(); updateStats(); return; } try { const snapshot = await db.collection("users") .doc(currentUser.uid) .collection("ownedCoins") .get(); ownedCoinsCache = []; snapshot.forEach(doc => { ownedCoinsCache.push({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }); }); renderMyCoins(); updateStats(); } catch (error) { console.error("Load owned coins error:", error); showToast("Unable to load your coins.", "error"); } } function getAssetById(assetId) { return assetsCache.find(asset => asset.id === assetId || asset.coinID === assetId ); } function getListingAsset(listing) { return getAssetById( listing.assetId || listing.coinID ); } function coinImage(image, name) { if (image) { return ` `; } return `
${escapeHTML( safeText(name).charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "M" )}
`; } function renderFeaturedCoins() { const container = $("featuredCoins"); if (!container) return; const featured = assetsCache.slice(0, 6); if (!featured.length) { renderEmpty( container, "No MemeCoins have been issued yet." ); return; } container.innerHTML = featured.map(asset => coinCard(asset, null, true) ).join(""); } function renderMarketplace() { const container = $("marketplaceGrid"); if (!container) return; const search = state.search.toLowerCase().trim(); const selectedTier = state.selectedTier; let filtered = marketplaceCache.filter(listing => { const asset = getListingAsset(listing); if (!asset) return false; const text = ( asset.name + " " + asset.symbol + " " + asset.coinID ).toLowerCase(); const matchesSearch = !search || text.includes(search); const matchesTier = selectedTier === "all" || asset.tier === selectedTier || listing.tier === selectedTier; return matchesSearch && matchesTier; }); if (!filtered.length) { renderEmpty( container, "No active marketplace listings found." ); return; } container.innerHTML = filtered.map(listing => { const asset = getListingAsset(listing); return coinCard(asset, listing, false); }).join(""); } function coinCard(asset, listing = null, featured = false) { if (!asset) return ""; const price = listing ? listing.price : asset.primaryPrice; const fee = calculateBuyerFee(price); const total = calculateBuyerTotal(price); return `
${coinImage(asset.image, asset.name)} ${escapeHTML(tierLabel(asset.tier))}

${escapeHTML(asset.name)}

$${escapeHTML(asset.symbol)}
${money(price)}

${escapeHTML( asset.description || "Collectible MemeCoin asset." )}

ID: ${escapeHTML(asset.coinID)} ${escapeHTML( tierLabel(asset.tier) )}
${ listing ? ` ` : ` ` }
${ listing ? `
Seller ${escapeHTML( listing.sellerName )}
` : `
Buyer fee ${money(fee)}
` }
Total ${money(total)}
`; } function renderMyCoins() { const container = $("myCoinsGrid"); if (!container) return; if (!currentUser) { renderEmpty( container, "Sign in to view your owned MemeCoins." ); return; } if (!ownedCoinsCache.length) { renderEmpty( container, "You do not own any MemeCoins yet." ); return; } container.innerHTML = ownedCoinsCache.map(coin => { const asset = getAssetById( coin.assetId || coin.coinID ); const name = coin.customName || asset?.name || coin.name || "MemeCoin"; const image = coin.customImage || asset?.image || ""; const tier = coin.tier || asset?.tier || "New"; const value = Number( coin.currentValue ?? coin.value ?? asset?.primaryPrice ?? 0 ); return `
${coinImage(image, name)} ${escapeHTML(tierLabel(tier))}

${escapeHTML(name)}

$${escapeHTML( coin.symbol || asset?.symbol || "MEME" )}
${money(value)}

Owned since ${escapeHTML( formatDate(coin.acquiredAt) )}

`; }).join(""); } function renderMyListings() { const container = $("myListings"); if (!container) return; if (!currentUser) { renderEmpty( container, "Sign in to view your listings." ); return; } const listings = marketplaceCache.filter( listing => listing.sellerId === currentUser.uid ); if (!listings.length) { renderEmpty( container, "You have no active listings." ); return; } container.innerHTML = listings.map(listing => { const asset = getListingAsset(listing); const name = asset?.name || listing.coinID; return `
${escapeHTML(name)} ${escapeHTML(listing.coinID)}
${money(listing.price)} ${escapeHTML(listing.status)}
`; }).join(""); } function updateAdminCounts() { if (!isAdmin) return; const assets = $("adminAssetCount"); const listings = $("adminListingCount"); const transactions = $("adminTransactionCount"); if (assets) { assets.textContent = assetsCache.length.toLocaleString(); } if (listings) { listings.textContent = marketplaceCache.length.toLocaleString(); } if (transactions) { transactions.textContent = transactionsCache.length.toLocaleString(); } } function openAssetDetails(assetId) { const asset = getAssetById(assetId); const container = $("detailsContent"); if (!asset || !container) return; container.innerHTML = `
${coinImage(asset.image, asset.name)}
${escapeHTML(tierLabel(asset.tier))}

${escapeHTML(asset.name)}

$${escapeHTML(asset.symbol)}

${escapeHTML( asset.description || "No description available." )}

Coin ID ${escapeHTML(asset.coinID)}
Primary Price ${money( asset.primaryPrice )}
Supply ${escapeHTML( asset.supply || 0 )}
Created ${escapeHTML( formatDate(asset.createdAt) )}
`; openModal("detailsModal"); } function openPrimaryPurchase(assetId) { const asset = getAssetById(assetId); if (!asset) return; currentAsset = asset; currentListing = null; showPurchaseModal(asset, null); } function openPurchase(listingId) { const listing = marketplaceCache.find(item => item.id === listingId); if (!listing) return; const asset = getListingAsset(listing); if (!asset) return; currentAsset = asset; currentListing = listing; showPurchaseModal(asset, listing); } function showPurchaseModal(asset, listing) { const container = $("purchaseContent"); const button = $("confirmPurchaseBtn"); if (!container || !button) return; const price = listing ? listing.price : asset.primaryPrice; const fee = calculateBuyerFee(price); const total = calculateBuyerTotal(price); container.innerHTML = `
${coinImage(asset.image, asset.name)}

${escapeHTML(asset.name)}

$${escapeHTML(asset.symbol)}

Price ${money(price)}
Platform fee (1%) ${money(fee)}
Total ${money(total)}
Payment mode: ${escapeHTML(PAYMENT_MODE)}. Supported crypto: BTC, ETH, SOL and USDT.
`; button.onclick = confirmPurchase; openModal("purchaseModal"); } async function confirmPurchase() { if (!currentUser) { closeModal("purchaseModal"); await signInWithGoogle(); return; } if (!currentAsset) { showToast("No MemeCoin selected.", "error"); return; } const price = currentListing ? currentListing.price : currentAsset.primaryPrice; const fee = calculateBuyerFee(price); const total = calculateBuyerTotal(price); const button = $("confirmPurchaseBtn"); if (button) { button.disabled = true; button.textContent = "Processing..."; } try { const transactionId = generateTransactionId(); const transaction = { transactionId, assetId: currentAsset.id, coinID: currentAsset.coinID, buyerId: currentUser.uid, buyerName: currentUser.displayName || "User", sellerId: currentListing ? currentListing.sellerId : ADMIN_UID, sellerName: currentListing ? currentListing.sellerName : "MemeCoinSite", price, platformFee: fee, total, crypto: null, paymentMode: PAYMENT_MODE, status: "payment_pending", type: currentListing ? "secondary" : "primary", createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }; await db.collection("transactions") .doc(transactionId) .set(transaction); closeModal("purchaseModal"); showToast( "Purchase request created. Payment verification is pending.", "success" ); await loadTransactions(); } catch (error) { console.error("Purchase error:", error); showToast( "Unable to create purchase request.", "error" ); } finally { if (button) { button.disabled = false; button.textContent = "Confirm Purchase"; } } } async function loadTransactions() { if (!currentUser) { transactionsCache = []; return; } try { const snapshot = await db.collection("transactions") .where("buyerId", "==", currentUser.uid) .get(); transactionsCache = []; snapshot.forEach(doc => { transactionsCache.push({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }); }); transactionsCache.sort((a, b) => { const aTime = a.createdAt?.seconds || 0; const bTime = b.createdAt?.seconds || 0; return bTime - aTime; }); renderHistory(); } catch (error) { console.error("Load transactions error:", error); } } function renderHistory() { const container = $("historyContainer"); if (!container) return; if (!currentUser) { renderEmpty( container, "Sign in to view your transaction history." ); return; } if (!transactionsCache.length) { renderEmpty( container, "No transactions yet." ); return; } container.innerHTML = transactionsCache.map(tx => `
${escapeHTML(tx.coinID || "MemeCoin")} ${escapeHTML(tx.type || "transaction")}
${escapeHTML(formatDate(tx.createdAt))}
${money(tx.total)}
${escapeHTML(tx.status || "pending")}
`).join(""); } async function createAdminCoin() { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) { showToast("Admin access denied.", "error"); return; } const name = $("adminCoinName")?.value.trim(); const symbol = $("adminCoinSymbol") ?.value.trim() .toUpperCase(); const price = Number( $("adminCoinPrice")?.value || 0 ); const tier = $("adminCoinTier")?.value || "New"; const description = $("adminCoinDescription")?.value.trim() || ""; const image = $("adminCoinImage")?.value.trim() || ""; const supply = Number( $("adminCoinSupply")?.value || 0 ); if (!name || !symbol || price <= 0 || supply <= 0) { showToast( "Please complete all required fields.", "error" ); return; } if (!validTierPrice(tier, price)) { const range = getTierRange(tier); showToast( `Price for ${tier} must be between ${money(range[0])} and ${money(range[1])}.`, "error" ); return; } const coinID = generateCoinId(); const asset = { coinID, name, symbol, description, image, primaryPrice: price, tier, supply, availableSupply: supply, createdBy: ADMIN_UID, status: "active", createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }; try { await db.collection("assets") .doc(coinID) .set(asset); showToast( "MemeCoin created successfully.", "success" ); [ "adminCoinName", "adminCoinSymbol", "adminCoinPrice", "adminCoinDescription", "adminCoinImage" ].forEach(id => { if ($(id)) $(id).value = ""; }); if ($("adminCoinSupply")) { $("adminCoinSupply").value = 100; } await loadAssets(); } catch (error) { console.error( "Create coin error:", error ); showToast( "Unable to create MemeCoin.", "error" ); } } async function loadAdminData() { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) { showToast( "Admin access denied.", "error" ); return; } await Promise.all([ loadAssets(), loadMarketplace(), loadAdminTransactions() ]); updateAdminCounts(); } async function loadAdminTransactions() { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) return; try { const snapshot = await db.collection("transactions") .orderBy("createdAt", "desc") .limit(100) .get(); transactionsCache = []; snapshot.forEach(doc => { transactionsCache.push({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }); }); renderAdminTransactions(); updateAdminCounts(); } catch (error) { console.error( "Admin transaction error:", error ); const container = $("adminTransactions"); if (container) { renderEmpty( container, "Unable to load transactions." ); } } } function renderAdminTransactions() { const container = $("adminTransactions"); if (!container) return; if (!transactionsCache.length) { renderEmpty( container, "No transactions found." ); return; } container.innerHTML = transactionsCache.map(tx => `
${escapeHTML( tx.transactionId || tx.id )} ${escapeHTML(tx.coinID || "")}
${money(tx.total)}
${escapeHTML( tx.status || "pending" )}
${ tx.status === "payment_pending" ? ` ` : "" } ${ tx.status === "payment_pending" ? ` ` : "" }
`).join(""); } async function approveTransaction(transactionId) { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) { showToast( "Admin access denied.", "error" ); return; } try { await db.collection("transactions") .doc(transactionId) .update({ status: "approved", approvedBy: ADMIN_UID, approvedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); showToast( "Transaction approved.", "success" ); await loadAdminTransactions(); } catch (error) { console.error( "Approve transaction error:", error ); showToast( "Unable to approve transaction.", "error" ); } } async function rejectTransaction(transactionId) { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) { showToast( "Admin access denied.", "error" ); return; } try { await db.collection("transactions") .doc(transactionId) .update({ status: "rejected", rejectedBy: ADMIN_UID, rejectedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); showToast( "Transaction rejected.", "success" ); await loadAdminTransactions(); } catch (error) { console.error( "Reject transaction error:", error ); showToast( "Unable to reject transaction.", "error" ); } } async function loadAdminListings() { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) return; const container = $("adminListings"); if (!container) return; try { const snapshot = await db.collection("marketplace") .where("status", "==", "active") .get(); const listings = []; snapshot.forEach(doc => { listings.push( normalizeListing( doc.data(), doc.id ) ); }); if (!listings.length) { renderEmpty( container, "No active marketplace listings found." ); return; } container.innerHTML = listings.map(listing => `
${escapeHTML(listing.coinID)} ${escapeHTML(listing.sellerName)}
${money(listing.price)}
${escapeHTML(listing.status)}
`).join(""); } catch (error) { console.error( "Admin listing error:", error ); renderEmpty( container, "Unable to load listings." ); } } async function removeAdminListing(listingId) { if (!isCurrentAdmin()) { showToast("Admin access denied.", "error"); return; } try { await db.collection("marketplace") .doc(listingId) .update({ status: "removed", removedBy: ADMIN_UID, removedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); showToast("Listing removed.", "success"); await loadMarketplace(); await loadAdminListings(); } catch (error) { console.error("Remove listing error:", error); showToast("Unable to remove listing.", "error"); } } async function cancelListing(listingId) { if (!currentUser) { showToast("Please sign in first.", "error"); return; } try { const ref = db.collection("marketplace").doc(listingId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) { showToast("Listing no longer exists.", "error"); return; } const listing = snap.data(); if (listing.sellerId !== currentUser.uid) { showToast("You cannot cancel this listing.", "error"); return; } await ref.update({ status: "cancelled", cancelledAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); showToast("Listing cancelled.", "success"); await loadMarketplace(); } catch (error) { console.error("Cancel listing error:", error); showToast("Unable to cancel listing.", "error"); } } function openOwnedDetails(ownedId) { const coin = ownedCoinsCache.find(item => item.id === ownedId); const container = $("detailsContent"); if (!coin || !container) return; const asset = getAssetById( coin.assetId || coin.coinID ); const name = coin.customName || asset?.name || coin.name || "MemeCoin"; const symbol = coin.symbol || asset?.symbol || "MEME"; const tier = coin.tier || asset?.tier || "New"; const value = Number( coin.currentValue ?? coin.value ?? asset?.primaryPrice ?? 0 ); container.innerHTML = `
${coinImage( coin.customImage || asset?.image || "", name )}
${escapeHTML(tierLabel(tier))}

${escapeHTML(name)}

$${escapeHTML(symbol)}

Coin ID ${escapeHTML( coin.coinID || asset?.coinID || "" )}
Current Value ${money(value)}
Acquired ${escapeHTML( formatDate(coin.acquiredAt) )}
Design Updated ${escapeHTML( formatDate(coin.updatedAt) )}
`; openModal("detailsModal"); } function openCustomize(ownedId) { if (!currentUser) { showToast("Please sign in first.", "error"); return; } const coin = ownedCoinsCache.find(item => item.id === ownedId); if (!coin) { showToast("Owned MemeCoin not found.", "error"); return; } currentAsset = getAssetById( coin.assetId || coin.coinID ); $("customName").value = coin.customName || currentAsset?.name || ""; $("customBackground").value = coin.customBackground || "#111722"; $("customTextColor").value = coin.customTextColor || "#ffffff"; $("customTagline").value = coin.customTagline || ""; $("customizeModal").dataset.ownedId = ownedId; openModal("customizeModal"); } async function saveCustomization() { if (!currentUser) { showToast("Please sign in first.", "error"); return; } const modal = $("customizeModal"); const ownedId = modal?.dataset.ownedId; if (!ownedId) { showToast("No coin selected.", "error"); return; } const ref = db.collection("users") .doc(currentUser.uid) .collection("ownedCoins") .doc(ownedId); const updates = { customName: $("customName")?.value.trim() || "", customBackground: $("customBackground")?.value.trim() || "#111722", customTextColor: $("customTextColor")?.value.trim() || "#ffffff", customTagline: $("customTagline")?.value.trim() || "", updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }; try { await ref.update(updates); await db.collection("ownershipHistory") .add({ ownedCoinId: ownedId, ownerId: currentUser.uid, assetId: currentAsset?.id || "", coinID: currentAsset?.coinID || ownedId, action: "design_updated", design: updates, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); closeModal("customizeModal"); showToast( "MemeCoin design saved.", "success" ); await loadOwnedCoins(); } catch (error) { console.error( "Customization error:", error ); showToast( "Unable to save customization.", "error" ); } } function openSell(ownedId) { if (!currentUser) { showToast("Please sign in first.", "error"); return; } const coin = ownedCoinsCache.find( item => item.id === ownedId ); if (!coin) { showToast("Owned MemeCoin not found.", "error"); return; } const asset = getAssetById( coin.assetId || coin.coinID ); if (!asset) { showToast("Asset information unavailable.", "error"); return; } const currentValue = Number( coin.currentValue ?? coin.value ?? asset.primaryPrice ?? 0 ); const priceInput = prompt( `Enter selling price in USD.\nSuggested value: ${money(currentValue)}` ); if (priceInput === null) return; const price = Number(priceInput); if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0) { showToast("Enter a valid selling price.", "error"); return; } createMarketplaceListing( ownedId, coin, asset, price ); } async function createMarketplaceListing( ownedId, coin, asset, price ) { try { const existing = await db.collection("marketplace") .where("sellerId", "==", currentUser.uid) .where("ownedCoinId", "==", ownedId) .where("status", "==", "active") .get(); if (!existing.empty) { showToast( "This MemeCoin is already listed.", "error" ); return; } const listingId = generateListingId(); const listing = { listingId, assetId: asset.id, coinID: asset.coinID, ownedCoinId: ownedId, sellerId: currentUser.uid, sellerName: currentUser.displayName || currentUser.email || "User", price, tier: coin.tier || asset.tier || "New", status: "active", createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }; await db.collection("marketplace") .doc(listingId) .set(listing); await db.collection("users") .doc(currentUser.uid) .collection("ownedCoins") .doc(ownedId) .update({ listingId, listed: true, listingPrice: price, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); const transactionSnap = await transactionRef.get(); if (!transactionSnap.exists) { showToast( "Transaction not found.", "error" ); return; } const tx = transactionSnap.data(); if (tx.status !== "approved") { showToast( "Transaction must be approved first.", "error" ); return; } const buyerId = tx.buyerId; const sellerId = tx.sellerId; const assetId = tx.assetId; const assetRef = db.collection("assets") .doc(assetId); const assetSnap = await assetRef.get(); if (!assetSnap.exists) { showToast( "Asset not found.", "error" ); return; } const asset = assetSnap.data(); const ownedCoinId = `${tx.coinID}_${buyerId}`; const ownershipRef = db.collection("users") .doc(buyerId) .collection("ownedCoins") .doc(ownedCoinId); const ownershipHistoryRef = db.collection("ownershipHistory") .doc(); if (ownershipSnap.exists) { throw new Error( "COIN_ALREADY_OWNED" ); } const buyerSnap = await runner.get(buyerUserRef); const buyerData = buyerSnap.data() || {}; const sellerSnap = await runner.get(sellerUserRef); const sellerData = sellerSnap.data() || {}; const buyerCoins = Number( buyerData.coinsOwned || 0 ); const sellerCoins = Number( sellerData.coinsOwned || 0 ); runner.set( ownershipRef, { assetId, coinID: tx.coinID, name: asset.name || "MemeCoin", symbol: asset.symbol || "MEME", tier: asset.tier || "New", value: Number( tx.price || asset.primaryPrice || 0 ), currentValue: Number( tx.price || asset.primaryPrice || 0 ), ownerId: buyerId, listed: false, acquiredAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() } ); runner.update( buyerUserRef, { coinsOwned: buyerCoins + 1, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() } ); if (sellerId && sellerId !== ADMIN_UID) { runner.update( sellerUserRef, { coinsOwned: Math.max(0, sellerCoins - 1), updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() } ); } runner.set( ownershipHistoryRef, { coinID: tx.coinID, assetId, ownerId: buyerId, previousOwnerId: sellerId || ADMIN_UID, action: "ownership_transferred", transactionId, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() } ); runner.update( transactionRef, { status: "completed", completedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), completedBy: ADMIN_UID, ownershipId: ownedCoinId } ); if (tx.type === "secondary" && tx.listingId) { runner.update( db.collection("marketplace") .doc(tx.listingId), { status: "sold", soldTo: buyerId, soldTransactionId: transactionId, soldAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() } ); } } ); sellerSnap.docs.forEach( doc=>{ const tx = doc.data(); if( tx.status === "completed" ){ sales++; } } ); const coins = ownershipSnap.size; const community = await getCommunityScore( userId ); const score = coins * 10 + sales * 20 + purchases * 10 + community + Math.min( volume, 10000 ); function getMarketplaceFilterValues(){ const search = document.getElementById("marketSearch"); const tier = document.getElementById("tierFilter"); return { search: search ? search.value.trim().toLowerCase() : "", tier: tier ? tier.value.trim().toLowerCase() : "all" }; } if( !marketplaceMatchesSearch( listing, values.search ) ){ return false; } if( !marketplaceMatchesTier( listing, values.tier ) ){ return false; } return true; } ); } function marketplaceSortListings( listings, mode ){ const result = Array.isArray(listings) ? [...listings] : []; if(mode === "price-low"){ return result.sort( function(a,b){ return ( getListingPrice(a) - getListingPrice(b) ); } ); } if(mode === "price-high"){ return result.sort( function(a,b){ return ( getListingPrice(b) - getListingPrice(a) ); } ); } if(mode === "newest"){ return result.sort( function(a,b){ const aTime = getMarketplaceTimestamp( a ); const bTime = getMarketplaceTimestamp( b ); return bTime - aTime; } ); } if(mode === "oldest"){ return result.sort( function(a,b){ const aTime = getMarketplaceTimestamp( a ); const bTime = getMarketplaceTimestamp( b ); return aTime - bTime; } ); } return result; } function getMarketplaceTimestamp( listing ){ if(!listing){ return 0; } const value = listing.createdAt || listing.listedAt || listing.updatedAt; if( value && typeof value.toMillis === "function" ){ return value.toMillis(); } if( value && value.seconds ){ return ( Number(value.seconds) * 1000 ); } const parsed = Date.parse( String(value || "") ); return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0; } function ensureMarketplaceSortControl(){ const search = document.getElementById( "marketSearch" ); const tier = document.getElementById( "tierFilter" ); if(!search && !tier){ return; } if( document.getElementById( "marketSort" ) ){ return; } const control = document.createElement( "select" ); control.id = "marketSort"; control.innerHTML = ` `; const parent = ( search && search.parentElement ) || ( tier && tier.parentElement ); if(parent){ parent.appendChild( control ); } } function getMarketplaceSortMode(){ const control = document.getElementById( "marketSort" ); return control ? function normalizeAuditLog(log){ const data = log || {}; return { id: data.id || data.logId || "", transactionId: data.transactionId || "", action: data.action || data.type || "unknown", adminId: data.adminId || data.actorId || "", adminName: data.adminName || data.actorName || "Administrator", details: data.details || data.reason || "", status: data.status || "", createdAt: data.createdAt || data.timestamp || null };
